- **MARATHON-2026-07-27 (gate + fleet integrity) fired: 4 sequential lanes, all approved, PR #318.** `gh311 → gh289 → gh293 → gh308`, builder codex / reviewer agy throughout, 2 turns per lane (4 for gh293). Each deliverable was verified by direct inspection of the diff against its brief rather than by the runner's summary line — which is how GH-319 surfaced. **GH-311** adds `test/pdda-repo-contract.sh`, running `utils/pdda/pdda.sh run` against the repo's real docs so `validate.sh` becomes a superset of tier1's PDDA contract; it immediately earned its place by catching two real errors in this changelog's own capture doc (a hardcoded `$HOME` path and a missing ROADMAP pointer). **GH-289** widens GH-245's fast-refusal from `--review-once` to every `--target-root` turn, so a BUILD turn refuses at startup instead of completing and discarding its Log; the `--review-once` diagnostic is preserved verbatim. **GH-293** adds a behavior-marker safety-guard manifest to `xyz-sync check` (a missing guard is now distinguishable from ordinary provenance drift, inspected in the installed copy rather than inferred from `source_commit`) and makes `update --all` refuse a dirty or non-canonical harness source without `XYZ_SYNC_ALLOW_UNSAFE_SOURCE=1`. **GH-308 Phase 1** stamps FROZEN banners on the 11 Tier-A Bash twins and records the policy in AGENTS.md/UPGRADE.md — no deletions, `XYZ_PYTHON=0` still reversible, with `utils/marathon-plan.sh` and `relay-turn-lib.sh` explicitly exempt. `relay-pkg.tar.gz` rebuilt (the freeze banners drift it). → [MARATHON.yaml](PROJECT/2-WORKING/MARATHON-2026-07-27-GATE-AND-FLEET-INTEGRITY/MARATHON.yaml) · [#318](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/pull/318)
- **GH-312: `xyz-sync update` / `xyz-vendor.sh` silently destroyed the target repo's live runtime state — fixed by preserving it across the vendor swap, with three more at-risk paths than the report identified.** `materialize_vendor()` builds a staged mirror of the harness and swaps it in over an unconditional `rm -rf "$VENDOR_DIR"`. The stage is mirrored purely from `$HARNESS_ROOT`, so everything the *target* accumulated at runtime was deleted unread — and since `ensure_gitignore` keeps `.xyz/` out of git, nothing under it was ever hashed into a git object, leaving **no reflog, stash, or `git fsck --lost-found` recovery**. It exited 0 and printed success. A real incident on 2026-07-27 destroyed a completed two-round Codex relay thread plus its full `.tick/` event log; the findings survived only because the Codex CLI transcripts happen to live in `$TMPDIR`, outside the harness. **The intake report's requested fix was incomplete:** it named `relay-system/`, `.tick/`, and `.relay-driver.lock`, but auditing what `.xyz/` actually accumulates (repo `.gitignore` + GH-75) turned up the completion-telemetry family — `XYZ.json` (the locked record array, 1.8 MB and actively written on the reporting machine), `XYZ.json.lock/` (its `mkdir` advisory lock), and `XYZ.heartbeat.json` — with **identical exposure**: none are in `VENDOR_DIRS`, so the stage never recreated them either. Implementing the report literally would have left the bug half-fixed; the shipped preserve list carries **six** paths, and a failed copy now `die`s *before* the destructive swap rather than proceeding. Preservation was chosen over a warning or a refusal because both still depend on an operator reading output at the right moment, and the docs frame `update` as deliberate (pinned + manual) but never as destructive. **Regression coverage was written first and observed failing** — new `test/gh312-vendor-preserves-state.sh` produced 6 failures against the pre-fix code and passes 14/14 post-fix — and it pins the *other* half of the contract too: VERSION must still restamp to live HEAD and `bin/tick` must survive, so a "fix" that preserved state by skipping the update cannot pass. It also covers the direct `xyz-vendor.sh` re-run over an existing `.xyz/`, which the capture flagged as an unverified open question and which had the same destructive swap (**answered: yes**). Docs: `xyz-sync.sh`'s header and `skills/relay-xyz/SKILL.md` now state what `update` does to runtime state and that a new `.xyz/` artifact must join the preserve list. Reviewed by **agy** through a driven relay (`--review-once`, `ALLOW_PATHS=""`): Approved with 4 `[Pass]` findings and no defects raised — disclosed as a clean but shallow signal, not independent corroboration. `skills/relay-automation/relay-pkg.tar.gz` rebuilt via `make-pkg.sh` (the fix touches `relay-automation/`, which drifts the vendored tarball); `relay-pkg-freshness.sh` 3/3 and full `./validate.sh` green (10 passed / 0 failed / 1 skipped). **Deliberately still open:** whether durable runtime state belongs under a disposable, gitignored `.xyz/` at all — the capture's own sharper question, listed in its `non_goals`, and **not** closed by this fix. → [GH-312-XYZ-SYNC-DESTROYS-STATE.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-312-XYZ-SYNC-DESTROYS-STATE.md) · [#312](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/312)
- **GH-304: relay-drive turn-taker prompt + worktree seeding used a CWD-relative relay-file path — broke same-repo vendored `.xyz/` installs for Codex.** Scaffolding/driving a relay from CWD=`.xyz` (the relay-xyz skill's documented `cd "$HARNESS"`) with a CWD-relative `--relay-file` embedded that raw string in the turn-taker's prompt and used it for worktree seeding. Since a same-repo vendored `.xyz/` has no `.git` of its own, the turn-taker's worktree roots at the *outer* repo top-level, where `relay-system/…` doesn't exist (the file is at `.xyz/relay-system/…`) — a 100% failure for Codex, masked for agy (which absolutizes the path itself). Fix: `relay-drive.sh` and `utils/py/relay_drive.py` now **absolutize `$RELAY_FILE` before exporting it** to the turn-taker (mirrors the existing `--artifact-file` handling), so it resolves identically from any CWD; `rtl_turn_prompt` (`relay-turn-lib.sh`) derives the repo-root-relative EDIT path robustly (physical-path strip, matching `rtl_init`'s allowlist normalization) so the embedded path and the seeded path stay in lockstep at the true `.xyz/relay-system/…`. **Secondary:** when the relay file is gitignored (this repo gitignores parts of `relay-system/`), `rtl_enforce`'s empty-commit case now prints a distinct "relay file is gitignored, changes are NOT git-tracked (expected, not a stall)" message instead of the ambiguous "no tracked changes (token-only move?)" that a passing and a failing run printed byte-identically. Proof: new `test/gh304-vendored-relay-path.sh` (11/11) unit-tests the allowlist normalization, prompt path, worktree seeding, and both empty-commit messages; related relay/worktree tests green locally. → [#304](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/304)
- **GH-295: PR #302 tier1 CI — two real gaps found and fixed.** `test/pi-turn.sh`'s fake `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` test fixtures ("test-key"/"test-key-not-real") were never added to `security-scan-baseline.txt`, tripping the GH-64 active gate (same pattern already baselined for `aider-turn.sh`/`deep-research.sh`). Separately, `skills/relay-automation/relay-pkg.tar.gz` had drifted from an earlier README edit on this branch and was never rebuilt, tripping `test/relay-pkg-freshness.sh`. Both fixed; `security-scan.sh`/`relay-pkg-freshness.sh` green locally.
- **GH-300: swe-diagram search input no longer touches/overflows the font-picker when typing — two distinct bugs found and fixed, same symptom family.** **(1)** `.swe-search-input` uses `type="search"` but never reset `-webkit-appearance`, so WebKit's native searchfield cancel-button decoration could override the field's explicit 180px width once it had text — the renderer already ships its own custom `.swe-search-clear` button, making the native chrome pure downside. Fixed via `-webkit-appearance: none` + explicit `::-webkit-search-cancel-button`/`::-webkit-search-decoration` resets in `utils/swe-diagram/assets/template.html`. Not independently re-verified in Safari (no automation available in this environment) — a headless-Chrome regression check confirms no change there, since Chrome doesn't exhibit the underlying bug in the first place. **(2) Found on operator re-report** (the box still visibly touched the picker on the very first keystroke, no resize needed — but resizing afterward fixed it, the key clue): `positionPicker()` only ran on page load, font-picker change, and window resize — never on the search box's own `input` event — so when the custom clear button appeared (growing the right-anchored `.swe-search` row leftward), the picker never got told to move; a resize masked the bug by incidentally recomputing from the now-grown width. Fixed by having `renderer.js`'s search `input`/clear handlers dispatch a `swe-search-resize` event on the persistent `#diagram` node, which `template.html` listens for alongside its existing triggers. **Verified quantitatively, not just visually**: headless-Chrome bounding-rect measurement showed a healthy 8px gap before typing collapsing to a -22px overlap after typing (fix 1 alone), now a constant 8px with no resize needed — and since this is plain DOM sizing/event logic rather than a WebKit quirk, the Chrome verification here is directly conclusive. `test/swe-diagram.sh` 42/42 throughout; every `ARCHITECTURE/*.html` output rebuilt after each fix. → [GH-300-SWE-DIAGRAM-SEARCH-INPUT-OVERFLOW.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-300-SWE-DIAGRAM-SEARCH-INPUT-OVERFLOW.md) · [#300](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/300)

### Added
- **GH-299: new `trust-clustered` swe-diagram layout — the only one of 4 supported modes that clears all 5 readability acceptance criteria on the real architecture spec.** Baselined the real spec (`ARCHITECTURE/system-diagram.json`) under all 3 existing layouts first (layered/top-down/hub-ring — the latter two regenerated since they were stale, predating the 2026-07-09 trust-boundary additions): none cleared all criteria — hub-ring is structurally disqualified since group swimlanes are unsupported in that mode, and both hub-ring and top-down failed hub-dominance and label clarity. Per the plan's own escalation path, added `trustClusteredLayout()` to `utils/swe-diagram/assets/renderer.js`: bands nodes into 5 fixed columns derived from each node's existing `trust` field (Orchestration Spine / Turn-Shim Cluster / Untrusted Periphery / Shared State / Off-Live-Path Governance & Audit) rather than graph rank or author-chosen `group`, floats the containment core to the top of the spine column next to its dispatcher, wraps tiers past 6 members into sub-columns, and gives the containment-core node an emphasized border so it reads as a hub at a glance. Also added a generic edge-label-overlap avoidance pass (`placeLabelRect` in `drawEdges()`) after the baseline inspection found real label-on-label collisions in the busy periphery/shared-state boundary — a greedy vertical nudge against node rects + already-placed label rects, applied to all layouts, not just this one; re-screenshotted the other 3 layouts to confirm no regression. New sibling artifact `ARCHITECTURE/system-diagram-trust-clustered.json`/`.html` (additive; the default `system-diagram.html` is untouched). `test/swe-diagram.sh` 42/42 (10 new cases for `trustTierOf`/`trustClusteredLayout`). `./validate.sh` clean except 2 pre-existing, unrelated environmental reds (`acorn-extract.sh` missing the `acorn` npm module, `python:test_python_layer.py` 2/121 — neither touched by this change). → [GH-299-DIAGRAM-TRUST-LAYOUT.md](PROJECT/2-WORKING/GH-299-DIAGRAM-TRUST-LAYOUT.md) · [#299](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/299)

### Fixed
- **GH-296: `relay-drive.sh`/turn-shim EPERM on the tick lock in a same-repo vendored `.xyz/` install — root-caused as a regression via incomplete Python port, not a fresh instance of #263.** Captured via `/file-xyz-bug` from `pdda`; a Codex review turn driven against an artifact outside the vendored `.xyz/` failed to claim the tick token under both worktree-isolation settings. **#263's fix genuinely landed** in the Bash `relay-automation/codex-turn.sh` (commit `8c361a0`) — but this repo later flipped its default turn-shim runtime from Bash to Python (GH-264, `af7bb4d`), *after* #263 shipped, and `utils/py/codex-turn.py` — the file that actually executes by default now — never received the #263/GH-36 fix at all, and separately defaulted its own `root` variable to the harness's own directory (`XYZ_ROOT`) instead of falling back to the CWD's git toplevel the way the Bash version does. Confirmed via `git stash`/rerun: pre-fix, the new regression test reproduced the exact bogus `--add-dir <wrong>/.tick` sandbox path from the original bug report. Fixed: new `resolve_turn_root()` helper in `utils/py/rtl.py` (explicit override → CWD git toplevel → `XYZ_ROOT` fallback, mirroring Bash) used by `codex-turn.py`'s `root`; its `--add-dir` sandbox flag now builds off `tick_repo_root` unconditionally (both isolation states), porting #263's actual intent. **Follow-up folded into the same fix (not filed as a separate issue — same root cause, found while reviewing this one):** `agy-turn.py`/`claude-turn.py` shared the identical `root` defect, manifesting as their own pre-launch `tick claim --paths` call computing an escaping `../../../relay.md`-style path instead of a clean repo-relative one (the shared bridge into `relay-turn-lib.sh`'s worktree/containment logic already self-corrects a subdirectory root via the existing GH-160 fix, so this only bit the native, unbridged Python computation, `rtl.claim_paths_for_turn`). A second, independent bug surfaced while building the regression test for this: `git rev-parse --show-toplevel` returns the *physical* path (macOS resolves `/var`/`/tmp` symlinks to `/private/var`/`/private/tmp`), which could mismatch a caller-supplied `relay_file` still in unresolved logical form — the same GH-51 physical/logical-path class of bug `relay-turn-lib.sh` already guards against on the bridged side, ported natively into `claim_paths_for_turn` via `os.path.realpath()` on both sides of the comparison. 5 new/extended regression cases across `test/{codex,agy,claude}-turn.sh` — each independently verified to **fail against the pre-fix code and pass post-fix** via a `git stash`/rerun/`stash pop` cycle, not just written and trusted. Also caught and fixed along the way: editing `test/*.sh` drifted the bundled `skills/relay-automation/relay-pkg.tar.gz`, caught by `relay-pkg-freshness.sh` — rebuilt via `make-pkg.sh`. Full `validate.sh`: only the two long-standing, unrelated environmental reds remain (`acorn-extract.sh` missing the `acorn` npm module, `python:test_python_layer.py` missing `pytest`, neither installed in this local clone) — confirmed neither test file references any of the touched files. **Related, not verified:** the fix may also resolve the open #272 (same `TICK_REPO_ROOT`-in-vendored-lane root-cause family, a different symptom — a successful turn whose `tick release` lands in the wrong namespace) — flagged in the doc for a human to confirm separately. → [GH-296-RELAY-DRIVE-TICK-EPERM.md](PROJECT/1-INBOX/GH-296-RELAY-DRIVE-TICK-EPERM.md) · [#296](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/296) · [#263](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/263)
- **GH-279: two real in-repo gaps from the aider-qwen marathon trial fixed — zero-byte/unchanged artifacts no longer pass as builder progress, and the aider-turn API key no longer leaks into `ps` argv.** Closes out the GH-279/GH-280 marathon: re-grounded all 6 original punch-list items against the live repo before scoping (items 1/2 resolved via #280, item 3 tracked separately as #278, items 5 and 6b confirmed **not applicable** — no in-repo failure-tally/watchdog mechanism exists anywhere, and `timeout(1)` portability is already solved via `relay-turn-lib.sh`'s `rtl_run_bounded()`). Only two real gaps remained. **(4)** `artifacts_exist_for_timeout()` (`marathon-drive.sh`) / `artifacts_exist()` (`marathon_drive.py`) checked file existence only, so a killed/no-op builder turn's 0-byte pre-created stub passed as "artifact appeared" — fixed by generalizing the existing `requires_test_delta()` non-empty+git-delta pattern (`path_has_nonempty_phase_delta()`) to the artifact-appeared check too, in both runtimes, preserving `--requires-test`'s existing contract byte-for-byte. **(6a)** `aider-turn.sh`/`aider-turn.py`'s `AIDER_OPENAI_API_BASE` seam passed the API key as a literal `--openai-api-key` CLI argument, visible to any local `ps` call — fixed by passing it via the `OPENAI_API_KEY` environment variable instead (which Aider/LiteLLM reads natively), dropping the CLI flag. Fired via `swarm-preflight → marathon-drive` (Codex builder, agy reviewer). **Process note:** the first turn implemented both fixes correctly and added solid regression coverage (verified: key absent from captured argv AND present in the subprocess env; zero-byte and unchanged-artifact cases both correctly classified as no-progress), but also made two off-allowlist edits — a `CHANGELOG.md` entry and a rewrite of an unrelated, concurrently-open `utils/swe-diagram/NEW.md` scratch file — both correctly caught and reverted by containment (exit 6). A re-fire collided with `--require-clean` against the operator's own live edits on that same scratch file, so this phase was closed out manually rather than risk a third automated invocation: the already-correct in-scope diff was reviewed directly, `test/aider-turn.sh` (63/63) and `test/marathon-drive.sh` (137/137) run standalone, and a full `validate.sh` pass confirmed only the pre-existing `acorn-extract.sh`/`python:test_python_layer.py` environmental reds plus one confirmed-flaky `relay-turn-timeout.sh` (9/9 clean standalone). **Agy's independent review did not run** (auth expired in this environment) — reviewed by the orchestrating session instead of a second model; disclosed here rather than silently treated as equivalent. → [GH-279-AIDER-QWEN-MARATHON-TRIAL-FINDINGS.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-279-AIDER-QWEN-MARATHON-TRIAL-FINDINGS.md) · [#279](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/279)

### Added
- **GH-280: Aider+Qwen reliability investigation concluded — root cause found and fix confirmed.** A live GH-268 Wave-1 exercise against the real production repo and the real `qwen3.8-max-preview` endpoint caught the first real failure transcript of the whole investigation: Aider auto-selects `whole` edit format for unlisted/custom model ids, but Qwen emits standard unified diffs regardless, which Aider silently discards as "no tracked changes" — the actual explanation for the historical ~86% aider-qwen failure rate, not general model unreliability. Re-running with `AIDER_FLAGS="--edit-format diff"` forced took the same task from 0/3 real edits to a 90%-complete, correct implementation landing in round 1, confirming the fix. Getting to this transcript required first closing a real observability gap: `aider-turn.sh` (and, since `XYZ_PYTHON` defaults on, the actually-executing `utils/py/aider-turn.py` and `utils/py/agy-turn.py`) were never using the GH-161 persistent-log helper, so every prior investigation round genuinely could not have captured a failure log — fixed in [#288](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/pull/288)/[#290](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/pull/290). Also found and fixed along the way, independent of Qwen: a fully-retired OpenRouter default model id (`openrouter/anthropic/claude-3.5-sonnet`) that silently broke any plain `aider`/`consult` call omitting both `AIDER_MODEL` and `AIDER_OPENAI_API_BASE` — [#291](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/pull/291), no separate issue filed. **Recommendation:** codex+agy remains the default builder pairing; Aider+Qwen is usable as a fallback with `--edit-format diff` and a generous timeout set explicitly. A separate, genuinely new gap was surfaced by the same session and filed on its own: `swarm-preflight.sh`'s suggested marathon invocations never default to `RELAY_WORKTREE_ISOLATION=1` — [#294](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/294) (filed, not yet fired). → [GH-280 findings](PROJECT/1-INBOX/GH-280-AIDER-QWEN-VS-SONNET-INVESTIGATION.md) · [marathon plan](PROJECT/2-WORKING/MARATHON-PLAN-2026-07-23-GH279-GH280-AIDER-QWEN-FOLLOWUP.md) · [#280](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/280)

Then, orchestrator-side, wired the six §1.3 `marathon-drive.sh` debug-capture hooks (opt-in `XYZ_DEBUG_LOG`, default OFF, byte-identical when off) with `test/sentinel-driver-hooks.sh`. On an operator design call, built the **full Tier-2 triage overlay** (`sentinel-overlay/`: reader → local-Gemma classify → draft PDDA doc → file issue → nightly batch → PR-emit → adversarial red-team → morning report) as **visible in-repo reference code** for transparency, with all call-home gated behind a **gitignored `runtime.env`** so a downstream clone is completely inert (no network/LLM/GitHub) until the operator opts in — enforced by a single `sentinel_active` gate, egress-only wrappers, a static guard, and `test/sentinel-overlay.sh` proving zero egress with no config. Full `validate.sh` green after both fixes. **All 5 phases of GH-273 are now shipped; doc moved to `3-COMPLETED`, issue closed.** A real harness bug found along the way (same-phase-id retry clobbers a phase's Approved record) was split out and filed separately as [#274](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/274) — not fired, tracked as its own follow-on. → [#273](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/273) · [GH-273-MARATHON-CLOSEOUT-AUTOMATION.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-273-MARATHON-CLOSEOUT-AUTOMATION.md)
- **GH-273: Phase 3 SHIPPED — `relay-automation/marathon-closeout.sh` extracts the deterministic post-marathon git ceremony into one script.** Fired via `swarm-preflight → marathon-drive` with `--phase-id gh273-phase3`. Given this phase can `git push`/create/merge a PR — materially higher stakes than Phases 0-2's prompt/config files — the contract required `--dry-run` as a first-class mode and mandated the test suite stub `git`/`gh` (never touch this repo's real remote or GitHub during the build/test turn); verified both directly before closing out, not just trusted the gate. `marathon-closeout.sh`: stage+commit+push → `gh pr create` → `gh pr checks` (red → exit 4, halt before merge) → `gh pr view --json mergeable` (not `MERGEABLE` → exit 4, halt) → `gh pr merge` → switch to `development` → `git pull --ff-only`; command failures normalize to exit 3, argument/precondition errors to exit 2. `test/marathon-closeout.sh` PATH-shadows `git`/`gh` with fake binaries inside a disposable `mktemp` scratch repo — 18/18 pass (dry-run inertness, happy path, red-checks halt, unmergeable halt, command-failure/usage exit codes, `bash -n` clean), confirmed standalone. The dogfood itself proved the port sound IRL on the copy: locator green, `TICK_REPO_ROOT` correctly at the consumer root (not `.xyz/`), exit-code parity on error paths, and the swarm-preflight gap reproducing identically to root HEAD (88/8). → [#255](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/255) · [#261](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/261) · [#260](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/260)

### Added
- **Runtime triage labels for the post-flip dual-runtime harness — `runtime:python` / `runtime:bash` / `runtime:parity` created in the repo, and `/file-xyz-bug` now harvests+applies them.** Since the `XYZ_PYTHON` flip a harness bug can live in the Python twin (default), the legacy Bash opt-out path (`XYZ_PYTHON=0`), or as a *divergence* between the two (`runtime:parity` — e.g. `agy-turn.py` failing open where `agy-turn.sh` exits 5). `skills/file-xyz-bug/SKILL.md` gains a Runtime harvest row (a `.py` traceback ⇒ python, an inline `line NNN:` shell error ⇒ bash, differing behavior at the same commit ⇒ parity), a `--label "runtime:*"` step in `gh issue create` (omit rather than guess if unclear), and a Runtime line in the capture doc's Environment block. Labels only — no code path changed.
- **GH-255: `UPGRADE.md` hardened by a SECOND independent `/relay-xyz` review — Codex, 5 rounds to Approved, 9 real blockers + 6 shoulds fixed that the agy pass missed.** Ran Codex as a different-model reviewer over the already-agy-Approved doc; it found substantially more, all verified against the repo before fixing. **Blockers:** the `python3 --version` "gate" didn't enforce the >=3.8 floor (3.7 passed) → executable predicate; `git fetch origin <branch>` could leave `origin/<branch>` stale → bare `git fetch origin`; the locator was referenced by a non-existent root path → concrete `skills/relay-xyz/find-harness.sh` (+ `.xyz/` variant); **Codex caught a bug in one of my own fixes** — a `python3 -c … || { echo; }` guard whose `||` swallowed the failure and continued → rewrote §2 as a real accumulator `preconditions.sh` that fails closed (behaviorally proven); per-copy permanent rollback that `git -C <root> revert`ed the *shared* live root → isolated `git worktree add <flip-sha>^` re-vendor; the §2(c) parity gate pointed at GH-255 instead of being runnable → embedded a `comm -13` Python-attributable-failure computation; the gate could **false-pass** if `validate.sh` aborted before its summary (empty set looks clean) → footer-completion guard; the Phase-2 shim guard checked python3 *presence* but not the *>=3.8 version* → version-enforcing guard; and the Type-B (vendored leaf) path was internally non-executable (told to skip only Phases 1 & 3 while Phases 2 & 4 are also root-only) → leaf now runs **none** of Phases 1–4, receive-and-verify only. **The deepest correction:** the interpreter matrix claimed the 10 non-marathon-plan twins were "python3 only" — but `bin/tick` is a Node program (`bin/tick:1`) that **both** the Bash drivers (`relay-drive.sh:42`) and the Python twins shell to, so Node was always a whole-harness baseline and the "flip 10 without Node" escape hatch was incoherent; removed it. **Shoulds** covered the honest external-tool deps (`date`/`sed`/`bash`/`consult.sh`), the `xyz-sync check` semantics (metadata-only drift, so don't hand-edit `.xyz/` because the next `update` overwrites it — not because `check` catches it), and propagating the Node/marathon-plan/Type-B corrections consistently across §0/§9/§10. Final Codex verdict: 4× `[Pass]`, 0 open. **Process note:** the closing turn hit driver exit 6 (GH-141) because I hand-edited the tree mid-review; the containment guard reverted it and the orphan-backup mechanism preserved the content — re-applied in the close commit. Thread: `relay-system/2026-07-20/upgrade-doc-review-codex.md`. → [#255](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/255)
- **GH-255: `UPGRADE.md` — end-to-end reversible runbook for flipping the `XYZ_PYTHON` default (Bash → Python), QA'd by an agy `/relay-xyz` review to Approved in 2 rounds.** A repo-agnostic runbook covering the whole cutover so it doesn't become a one-more-thing stream: the three-lever reversibility model (runtime `XYZ_PYTHON=0` / `git revert` the isolated flip commit / re-vendor a consumer), the `${VAR:-0}`→`${VAR-0}` empty-string trap (colon-dash substitutes on empty *and* unset, so after the flip anyone clearing the var to "turn it off" silently gets Python — verified empirically), a `python3`-presence guard, the single-purpose flip commit, docs/discoverability so rollback isn't tribal knowledge, fleet propagation via `xyz-sync`, soak/abort criteria, and a portability section. **agy review (`relay-system/2026-07-20/upgrade-doc-review.md`, driven headless via `relay-drive.sh --review-once`): r1 FAIL with 2 `[Blocker]` + 4 `[Pass]` → r2 Approved.** Both blockers were real and independently verified against the repo before fixing: (1) **hidden Node dependency** — `utils/py/marathon_plan.py:105-107` runs `node --version` and hard-exits if absent before delegating to `_marathon_plan_node.js`, so the marathon-plan entry point is Python+Node, not pure Python; added a `node` precondition + an interpreter matrix + a per-file "partial flip" escape hatch (flip the other 10, leave marathon-plan on Bash). (2) **vendored-consumer contradiction** — §9 told the operator to run the two-mode `validate.sh` sweep on a target the same section said has no `validate.sh`; root cause was conflating a full harness clone with a vendored `.xyz/` leaf, fixed by splitting **Type A (full clone — runs all phases)** vs **Type B (vendored leaf — skips parity derivation, inherits from the flipped root, verifies via its own smoke path + `xyz-sync check`)**, confirmed against two live `.xyz/` copies. The 4 `[Pass]` accuracy checks (exactly 11 shim entry points, `:-`/`-` semantics, `xyz-sync` subcommands, `TEST_SOFT_FAIL`) were independently re-verified on both sides. An adversarial re-review question ("does any *other* twin have a hidden runtime dep?") surfaced a `"node"` reference in `swarm_preflight.py:652` that proved to be a gate-command allowlist *string*, not a subprocess call — matrix confirmed correct, only marathon-plan hard-depends on Node. → [#255](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/255)

### Fixed
- **GH-255: `agy-turn.py` no longer fails open on an isolation breach — the GH-178 B1 grounding check was never ported, so Python silently accepted what Bash fails (exit 5).** Found by the first complete `XYZ_PYTHON=1` suite run. `relay-automation/agy-turn.sh:238-249` scans the agy transcript after `rtl_worktree_end` and fails the turn if it cites the **real repo root** instead of the throwaway worktree — an agent that escaped isolation. `utils/py/agy-turn.py` implemented the off-lane-edit half (exit 6) but omitted the grounding scan entirely, so `RELAY_WORKTREE_ISOLATION=1` was enforcing materially less under Python than under Bash. **Diagnosis rested on a differential, not a guess:** the two GH-183/GH-187 *false-positive* guard cases (tick-command narration, markdown `file://` citations) were already **passing** under Python while only the genuine-breach case failed — the signature of a check that is *absent* (negatives pass trivially) rather than mis-tuned. Falsified the alternative that the scan lived in `rtl.py` or elsewhere in `utils/py/` before porting: zero matches anywhere. Ported the block verbatim, preserving all four false-positive filters (`[trace] ` prefix, `TICK_REPO_ROOT=`, `file://`, `](`) and the transcript `[FAIL]` breadcrumb append. **Breadcrumb worth recording:** `agy-turn.sh` is the *only* turn shim carrying this check — `codex/claude/aider` have no equivalent in either language — so their green Python tests were consistent with the finding rather than contradicting it. Verified: `test/agy-turn.sh` green in **both** modes (the breach case now exits 5, both false-positive guards still pass, so the check is not over-firing), and the full `XYZ_PYTHON=1` suite moves 104/116 → 105/116 with no new failures. Bash unchanged. → [#255](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/255)

### Added
- **GH-258: `vendor-stack` skill — one-step XYZ harness + PDDA governance install into a target repo.** New `skills/vendor-stack/` (SKILL.md + `find-pdda.sh` + `install.sh`) orchestrates the two independent installers rather than reimplementing either: it vendors the XYZ harness into `<target>/.xyz/` via `relay-automation/xyz-vendor.sh`, then **asks** the operator whether to also install the PDDA doc-governance runtime and, if yes, runs the pdda clone's `install.sh` in observe mode. **Design fork the operator raised — prompt + PDDA path resolution live at the skill layer, not baked into `xyz-vendor.sh`:** that script is copied verbatim into every vendored `.xyz/` and runs headlessly from marathon/relay automation, so teaching it to find and run PDDA would ship dead cross-repo resolution logic to machines with no pdda clone and put an interactive prompt inside a must-stay-non-interactive script. Each tool keeps its own installer and its own per-user upgrade registry (`~/.config/xyz/registry.tsv`, `~/.config/pdda/registry.tsv`), so `xyz-sync check/update` and `pdda-sync status/push` carry updates forward independently — the skill just runs them in sequence. `find-pdda.sh` is a device-agnostic resolver mirroring `relay-xyz/find-harness.sh` (env override → harness sibling `<harness-parent>/pdda` → conventional `~/…/pdda` paths; no hardcoded machine path), and an unresolved pdda clone downgrades to XYZ-only with a note, never a hard error. **Verification:** `find-pdda.sh --check` resolves the pdda clone both via the harness-sibling rule and via `PDDA_REPO` override; `bash -n` clean on both scripts; XYZ was vendored into `hyper-pandas-python-stack` (`.xyz/` present, `bin/tick` runs, `.xyz/` gitignored, registry row written) as the original driving task. Retroactive intake: the skill was authored first, then issue #258 + the `1-INBOX` capture were filed to bring the work into PDDA compliance per the issue-first SOP. → [#258](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/258) · [GH-258-VENDOR-STACK-SKILL.md](PROJECT/1-INBOX/GH-258-VENDOR-STACK-SKILL.md)
- **GH-226: provenance-vs-summary-surface coordination decided — inventory + locked reporting contract + a draft two-lane implementation plan (Plan M), all docs-only, held for Jedi Wright sign-off.** Resolves the coordination gap Jedi raised: GH-211 reshaped the operator summary and GH-178 shipped only a narrow provenance stamp, so the "full provenance" pass risked reworking the human-facing layer twice. **Inventory built from `git show` of the shipped commits, not memory** (`91f2fda` for GH-211; `17c1dc4`/`a7f5b04`/`d85da37`/`54972e9`/`52a94ef` for GH-178). Three findings shaped the decision. (1) **Asymmetry:** GH-211 reworked *only* consult's summary (`skills/consult/SKILL.md`); relay's operator report lives in the external `giant-brains-claude-skills` repo and was never touched — so there is nothing to coordinate against there yet, which is why that repo boundary is deferred, not opened. (2) **The Q3 target signal already exists:** `consult.sh` already computes `FIRSTHAND_COUNT` + a prompt-echoed classifier into a `*.PROVENANCE.txt` sidecar (GH-235), surfaced only as a `warn()`, never rendered into the report — so the next pass is *surface + placement*, not a new classifier. (3) **Dual bash+Python surface:** every provenance mechanic exists in both `relay-automation/*.sh` and the `utils/py/*.py` parity ports, so any stamp change lands in two places. **Contract locked** per Jedi's thread answers (2026-07-20): firsthand-read-vs-operator-asserted is the primary key (prompt-only conclusions flagged `conditional`), cited-vs-uncited demoted to a secondary affordance; provenance renders in the Disagree/adjudication body + inline `Sorted categories` notes, **never the TLDR**; consult (advisor grounding) and relay (reviewer citation discipline) keep separate stamp contracts on a shared surface — a split the inventory confirms already holds in code. **Q4 decided one issue, two disjoint lanes** — Lane C (`skills/consult/SKILL.md`, `relay-automation/consult.sh`, `utils/py/consult.py`) and Lane R (`relay-automation/relay-turn-lib.sh` + `new-relay.sh`), only shared dependency the single-sourced `rtl_has_uncited_claim` predicate (Wave-1 deliverable Lane C consumes). Planning marathon Plan I closed (both phases done); fireable Plan M drafted and **held — not promoted, not fired**. **Honest caveat carried into Plan M:** the "signal already exists, just needs surfacing" premise is read from the code and its GH-235 comments, *not* confirmed by a live consult run — flagged for a quick live check before Wave 2. Docs: [GH-226 capture doc](PROJECT/1-INBOX/GH-226-PROVENANCE-SUMMARY-SURFACE-COORDINATION.md) (§ Inventory / § Decision), [Plan I](PROJECT/2-WORKING/MARATHON-PLAN-2026-07-17-I-PROVENANCE-SUMMARY-COORDINATION.md), [Plan M](PROJECT/2-WORKING/MARATHON-PLAN-2026-07-20-M-GH226-PROVENANCE-IMPL.md). Coordinates with #178/#211/#235. → [#226](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/226)
- **GH-255: `TEST_SOFT_FAIL=1` — first complete `XYZ_PYTHON=1` run of `validate.sh`; 10 test files fail *only* under Python, where the tracked blocker list held one.** The suite had never run to completion in Python mode. Two layers of fail-fast were conflated: `validate.sh`'s top level was *already* not fail-fast (it collects `FAILED`), but `test/_setup.sh`'s `fail()` called `exit 1` on the first failed assertion — the exact mechanism that hid [#223](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/223) behind [#215](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/215). Added opt-in `TEST_SOFT_FAIL=1` (default-off; fail-fast byte-identical when unset). **Second, non-obvious half:** most tests end in a bare `exit 0` — correct under fail-fast, a *false green* under soft-fail — so the `_setup.sh` EXIT trap now re-asserts non-zero when `FAIL > 0`, preserving `$?` verbatim when the flag is unset. Verified all three paths: passing test → 0, flag-unset failing test → exit 1 with one FAIL line, flag-on → exit 1 with all of them. **A same-commit Bash baseline was run and subtracted**, without which pre-existing failures get misattributed to the port: baseline 114/116 (2 files, 9 assertions) vs. Python 104/116 (12 files, 62), at HEAD `607dd7c`. Three findings outrank the rest. (1) **`agy-turn.py` fails open on containment** — `genuine breach should exit 5, got 0`; Bash fails the turn, Python silently accepts. A safety regression, since containment is what stops the GH-177-class incidents. (2) **Bash and Python `marathon-drive` fail in *disjoint* sets** — Bash fails GH-171/GH-172 (vendored chain, exit 6), Python passes those and instead fails GH-207/GH-238/`--require-clean`-in-a-linked-worktree; neither is a superset, so "port the remaining fixes forward" is the wrong model and the **union** needs reconciling. (3) **Fixing one gap reliably unmasks the next** — GH-215 → GH-223 → now #235: `consult.sh` is still red *after* GH-223 shipped, with 7 assertions covering the whole untported provenance surface (`FIRSTHAND_COUNT`/`ECHOED_COUNT`, `t.PROMPT.txt`). Four failing files (`debug-mantra.sh`, `marathon.sh`, `relay-artifact-file.sh`, `relay-turn-trace.sh`) have no Python twin at all — they fail because they drive `marathon-drive.sh`/`relay-drive.sh`, so the blast radius exceeds `utils/py/`. **Process note, recorded because it nearly shipped bad data:** the first sweep ran against a local `development` 31 commits behind origin that predated the GH-223 fix, and its results were filed before the staleness was caught; the issue body was invalidated and re-measured at merged HEAD. A clean `git status` says nothing about whether the branch is *current*. **Side finding:** the local baseline does not reproduce [#170](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/170)'s 9 pre-existing failures or [#232](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/232)'s ~12 — only `marathon-drive.sh` and a stale `relay-pkg.tar.gz` — so both look stale or ubuntu-specific. Supersedes the cutover criterion in [GH-172-CUTOVER-RECOMMENDATION.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-172-CUTOVER-RECOMMENDATION.md), which assumed a single blocker; proposed replacement is *no Python-attributable failures against a same-commit Bash baseline*. No production code changed — test harness only. → [#255](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/255)

### Fixed
- **GH-241: `MARATHON.example.yaml` now states that phases run strictly one at a time and that `depends_on` is scalar-only — docs-only; the report's first claim was partially falsified before the fix was scoped.** First bug filed *through* the new `/file-xyz-bug` skill (from `rebalance-OS`, vendored `.xyz/`), and the first to exercise the "verify the report, don't apply it" gate. **Claim (1) as filed — "nothing signals sequential execution", evidenced by a 0-match grep — does not hold:** line 3 already read "runs … **in depends_on order**; the chain **HALTS** on the first phase that fails". The grep was a false negative, searching the literal `"in order"` against the string `"in depends_on order"`. The DX complaint survives in a narrower form, which is what got fixed: the line documented *ordering* but not *exclusivity*, and "in depends_on order" is precisely how a dependency-ordered **parallel** scheduler (`make -j`) describes itself — so the new text states execution is strictly one-at-a-time and that `depends_on` **constrains** ordering rather than **creating** it, since restating "runs in order" would not move a reader who read that line and still concluded concurrency. **Claim (2) reproduced exactly** against a local two-phase fixture: `depends_on: [p1]` → `depends_on unknown phase '[p1]'`, scalar parses fine; root cause is the hand-rolled YAML reader not implementing flow sequences, so `[p1]` survives as a literal scalar into the phase-id lookup (`bin/marathon-yaml:102-105`). The field table now marks the field scalar-and-single with the exact failure text, and the header recommends `--dry-run` as the standard pre-fire step. **Consistency finding the issue didn't call out:** `depends_on` (scalar, single) and `artifact` (comma-separated, multi) are adjacent rows of the same field table carrying two different multiplicity conventions — `artifact` documented its own, `depends_on` did not, leaving the reader to infer from the neighbour. **Deliberately NOT taken: suggested fix (3)**, a `^\[.*\]$` guard in `bin/marathon-yaml` — that is a code change plus a regression test, not doc-only, and stays open as defence-in-depth (the docs fix stops the author writing the list form; the guard would catch the one who writes it anyway). Verified no runtime change: `node bin/marathon-yaml relay-automation/MARATHON.example.yaml` emits a byte-identical phase table before and after; `relay-pkg-freshness` 3/3 (the example is not a vendored path). → [#241](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/241)

A dependency-free local generator (`utils/swe-diagram/scripts/git-history-to-json.js`) reads local and remote-tracking branch refs and emits a capped, auditable diagram spec while documenting Git's inability to recover deleted branch names or squash/rebase provenance. Added a self-contained newest-20 example at `ARCHITECTURE/git-history-diagram.json`/`.html`. - **`/file-xyz-bug` — cross-repo bug front-door into this repo's `PROJECT/1-INBOX/`, QA'd by an agy `/relay-xyz` review (Approved, r3).** Lets a Claude Code session standing in *any other repo* file a harness bug where it will actually be triaged: harvest-first intake (reconstructs the failing command, error text, and caller repo from the live transcript before asking the operator anything, capped at 3 questions), a redaction step, one preview → one confirmation, then `gh issue create` + a `GH-<n>-*.md` capture + a `ROADMAP.md` queue park. Ships `find-xyz.sh`, a device-agnostic locator with no hardcoded machine path (env override → skill's own real location via symlink resolution → relay-xyz's `find-harness.sh` → current repo); it **deliberately rejects a vendored `.xyz/` copy**, which `find-harness.sh` prefers — a vendored copy has no `PROJECT/1-INBOX`, so a report filed there is unreadable. Two guardrails carry real incident weight: it writes **nothing** into the caller's repo (no breadcrumbs, no cached absolute paths), and it **never commits or pushes** the capture, because this clone is shared with marathon/relay drivers that can leave it dirty or parked on a `marathon/*` branch. **agy review (`relay-system/2026-07-18/file-xyz-bug-qa.md`): 3 `[Blocker]` + 2 `[Should]` + 1 `[Nit]` → 5 implemented, 1 declined on evidence.** Best catch: `$XYZ_*` exports from the locator don't survive to the next Claude Code Bash call (CWD persists, shell state does not), so a later `gh issue create --repo "$XYZ_SLUG"` would have expanded empty at the one moment it mattered — fixed with an explicit callout plus scrubbing all four bare references. **Declined** agy's third Blocker (that `status: Proposed (1-INBOX — not yet active)` violates a `status: Proposed` enum) as incorrect: no such enum exists — `PROJECT/PDDA.md:230` prescribes that exact string as the minimum frontmatter for a `GH-` inbox capture, `PDDA.md:456` documents `status:` as "free-text, unvalidated by design", and `GH-226` carries the long form in-tree; applying it would have moved the doc *away* from the contract. agy verified the citation and **retracted the finding** in r3. Also fixed by smoke-testing rather than review: `install.sh` reported success when `ln -s` failed under the Bash sandbox (`~/.claude` is write-denied), leaving the skill uninstalled while claiming otherwise. Locator verified from a foreign repo, a spaces-in-path repo, a non-git dir, and a bogus `XYZ_REPO` override; slug regex verified across all six GitHub URL forms.
- **GH-235: Consult A4 provenance v0 — "prompt-trace classifier for cited claims" (FIRSTHAND vs ECHOED) shipped, built by a live Codex↔agy relay after a live Codex+agy consult picked the scope.** Closes the shipped A4 slice's structural blind spot: the binary `NO FIRSTHAND VERIFICATION CITED` check (`consult.sh:310-336` via `rtl_has_uncited_claim()`) can't tell a citation an advisor found firsthand from one it echoed out of the operator's prompt — both look "cited." **Scope decided by dogfooding Consult:** a parallel Codex+agy consult (`relay-system/2026-07-17/a4-scope-181325/`) on the fork "4-category taxonomy vs. 2-category v0" converged independently on **v0**, both citing Principle 7 + the reuse tie-breaker; Codex read the actual code with exact line-cites, agy reached the same call. **Built by dogfooding the relay:** Codex (Producer) implemented across `consult.sh` (persist `PROMPT_TEXT`-only to `$RUN_DIR/${LABEL}.PROMPT.txt`; wire per-advisor `${LABEL}.${model}.PROVENANCE.txt` sidecar + a single ECHOED warn line gated on `uncited==0 && echoed>0`) and `relay-turn-lib.sh` (new read-only `rtl_classify_cited_claims()` sibling to `rtl_has_uncited_claim()`, reusing `RTL_CLAIM_WORD_RE`/`RTL_CITATION_RE` verbatim and keeping `/\[Pass\]/` inline to dodge the macOS-awk `-v` unescape trap), plus 20 `test/consult.sh` assertions — worktree-isolated, file-scoped commit `bfbd357`, no push. agy (Reviewer) returned **VERDICT: Approved** with 5 `file:line`-cited `[Pass]` findings. The existing `NO FIRSTHAND VERIFICATION CITED` stamp is unchanged (no regression). Independently re-verified rather than trusting self-reports: diff read + `test/consult.sh` 62/62 + `bash validate.sh` 115/115. **Honest limitation retained in-code:** exact/whitespace-normalized substring matching false-FIRSTHANDs when an advisor reformats a prompt citation (prompt "consult.sh lines 117-126" vs. answer `consult.sh:117`) — v0 accepts this; the INFERENCE/UNSUPPORTED-ASSERTED split, reconciliation backstop, and false-consensus detection stay deferred. **Two dogfood findings surfaced by the consult itself:** (1) both consult transcripts tripped the shipped binary stamp — correct for agy (an uncited `[Pass]`), but a **false-positive** on Codex's heavily-cited answer (one claim-word line in its long streamed reasoning had no citation within 3 lines), real evidence the current check over-fires on long transcripts; (2) the consult prompt contained `consult.sh:117` while Codex answered `relay-automation/consult.sh:117` — a live instance of the exact format-drift the classifier can't catch. **Relay note:** agy's review turn exited 6 (containment) because it left a stray `*.tmp` scratch file, off-allowlist for a relay-file-only review — the shim correctly reverted it and declined to auto-commit; agy's surviving Approved review was committed by hand (`6158eed`). Proposal + capture: [GH-178-A4-PROVENANCE-TAXONOMY-PROPOSAL.md](PROJECT/1-INBOX/GH-178-A4-PROVENANCE-TAXONOMY-PROPOSAL.md), [GH-235-CONSULT-A4-PROVENANCE-V0.md](PROJECT/1-INBOX/GH-235-CONSULT-A4-PROVENANCE-V0.md). Coordinates with #226. → [#235](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/235)

### Fixed
- **GH-233 wipe-prevention fixes hardened after an agy `/relay-xyz` QA review — 3 real guard/backup defects closed, 1 reviewer false positive rejected on evidence.** Drove a headless agy review (`relay-system/2026-07-17/gh233-prevention-qa.md`, exit 5 = changes-requested) over the four prevention changes. Every finding was verified against the real code before acting (each claimed bypass reproduced pre-fix, a 22-case block/allow matrix re-run post-fix): (1) **passthru-wrapper bypass** — `command`/`eval`/`env [VAR=x]`/`exec`/`time`/`nohup`/`nice` and `.`/`source` all slipped a sandboxed suite run past `gh177-sandbox-test-guard.sh`; now stripped/recognised so they block; (2) **cd bypass** — `cd test && bash hq-hardening.sh` slipped through; added cross-segment `cd`-into-`test/` tracking (chose scoped tracking over the reviewer's blanket regex, which would over-block any `bash foo.sh`); `cd src && bash build.sh` and `cd test && cd .. && bash build.sh` correctly still allowed; (3) **corrupt-bundle-trusted** — `git-bundle-snapshot.sh` now writes `.tmp` → `verify` → `mv`, so a killed run leaves no half-written bundle for rotation to trust. Nested `$(...)`/backtick and `xargs`/`find -exec` execution documented as accepted residuals (accidental-self-inflict threat model; the static `mktemp-trap-guard.sh` + CI are the backstops). **Declined** agy's 4th finding — that the second `"matcher": "Bash"` hook block is "shadowed" and dead — as a verified false positive: official Claude Code docs confirm all matching matcher blocks run in parallel and are deduplicated, with no first-match short-circuit (and the guard self-noops on non-Bash events anyway). agy's AGENTS.md finding was a Pass. Static mktemp-trap audit stays green at 198 scripts.

### Added
- **GH-233 wipe-prevention bundle: three of four layers landed against a GH-177 recurrence.** (1) `relay-automation/hooks/gh177-sandbox-test-guard.sh` — a PreToolUse hook (registered in `.claude/settings.json`) that blocks EXECUTING `validate.sh`/`test/*.sh` under a sandboxed Claude Code Bash call, since sandbox-broken `mktemp` was the ignition for both wipes; keys on the trigger, not the payload (a deny-list can't see the `rm -rf` born inside a nested script). Non-executing references (`bash -n`, shellcheck, grep) and unsandboxed runs stay allowed; fail-open on parse errors, same convention as `relay-xyz-guard.sh`; verified against 8 block/allow cases. (2) AGENTS.md rail: tree-modifying Agent/Workflow subagents get `isolation: "worktree"` — with the honest caveats that worktrees share `.git` refs/objects (destructive git ops still hit the real repo, as the `rtl_enforce` ROOT-HEAD incident proved) and harness lanes are covered by `rtl_worktree_begin` instead. (3) `utils/git-bundle-snapshot.sh` + daily 09:00 cron: rotated `git bundle --all` snapshots to `~/Backups/xyz-3-agents-swarm/` (first bundle written + `git bundle verify`d, 8.7M; keep-14 rotation), so a future recovery is `git clone <bundle>` instead of an evening of reflog surgery. Layer (4), the vendored-copy sweep, was delegated to a read-only Opus 4.8 subagent and came back decisive: **5 of the 6 vendored `.xyz/` copies still carried the live wipe idiom** — the exact unguarded `TMP="$(cd "$(mktemp -d)" && pwd -P)"; trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT` line in `test/hq-hardening.sh:15`/`hq-locator.sh:18`/`hq-promote.sh:18`, 15 instances total (only LTVera-Pandas was clean, its older snapshot predating those tests). Remediated via the sanctioned path — `relay-automation/xyz-sync.sh update --all` re-vendored all 6 from the current fixed harness — and verified post-update: zero live instances remain (the only remaining grep hits are the fix's own explanatory comments), guarded `hq-*` tests 3/3 and `test/mktemp-trap-guard.sh` now ship in every copy. Side finding: every copy ships `find-harness.sh --env` with the known root-one-directory-too-deep bug noted-but-unfiled during the GH-177 investigation — now filed as [#234](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/234). All four layers done; [#233](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/233) closed.

### Fixed
- **GH-230 remediation executed: Quickstart now documents `npm install`, `package.json` drift cleaned, CI gained a fresh-clone safety net.** Phase 1: README Quickstart adds `npm install` immediately before `./validate.sh` (with a one-line note naming the `Cannot find module 'acorn'` failure it prevents); `package.json` renamed `lane-169` → `xyz-3-agents-swarm`, the garbled beta-banner `description` replaced with a real one-liner, and the deliberately-failing `npm test` stub now runs `./validate.sh`. Phase 2: CI `tier1` gained a final step running `npm ci` (a tracked `package-lock.json` already existed) + `bash test/acorn-extract.sh` — the one test that requires `node_modules` — so a missing-install-step regression fails in CI instead of on a newcomer's machine. (First attempt ran the full `./validate.sh` in CI; PR [#231](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/pull/231)'s run exposed ~12 pre-existing ubuntu-runner environment failures unrelated to this gap — `acorn-extract.sh` itself passed — now tracked in [#232](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/232); scoped the step down to the plan's own stated minimum.) Verified: `package.json` valid JSON, `ci.yml` valid YAML, `validate.sh` exec bit intact, `pdda.sh run` all-clean. Deliberately did NOT run `validate.sh` locally — a sandboxed local run is the exact GH-177 wipe vector; the new CI step is the exercise path. Remaining before closing [#230](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/230): CI triggers only on `main` push/PR, so the new step first fires on the next `development` → `main` PR (Phase 2 QA gate). Plan doc: [GH-230-FRONT-DOOR-ONBOARDING-DRIFT.md](PROJECT/2-WORKING/GH-230-FRONT-DOOR-ONBOARDING-DRIFT.md).

### Added
- **GH-230 filed: read-only `/front-door` audit found the repo's own Quickstart has an undocumented `npm install` step, plus `package.json` drift.** Cold-newcomer audit (read-only — no scripts executed, per the `audit-tasks-stay-read-only` lesson from GH-177) walked README's Quickstart verbatim. Verdict: ⚠️ Bumpy, no leaked secrets. `package.json` declares real dependencies (`acorn`, `acorn-walk`) that `src/acorn-extract.js` requires, wired into `validate.sh` via `test/acorn-extract.sh` (GH-169); `node_modules/` is gitignored and no doc anywhere mentions `npm install`, so a genuinely fresh clone hits `Cannot find module 'acorn'`. CI's own `tier1` job never runs `./validate.sh` either (only shellcheck/syntax/PDDA-gate), so the gap is invisible to CI. Also found: `package.json`'s stale `"name": "lane-169"`, a garbled beta-banner copy-paste as `"description"`, and a deliberately-failing `npm test` stub. Filed as [#230](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/230), remediation plan captured in [GH-230-FRONT-DOOR-ONBOARDING-DRIFT.md](PROJECT/2-WORKING/GH-230-FRONT-DOOR-ONBOARDING-DRIFT.md) (Phase 1: quick-win doc/manifest fixes; Phase 2: CI safety net so this class of regression can't recur invisibly). Not yet fixed.

### Added
- **GH-177 follow-up: `/relay-xyz` review with Codex hardened `test/mktemp-trap-guard.sh` through 5 rounds, escalated at the cap, one more operator-approved fix closed it.** Ran a cli-driven relay (`relay-system/2026-07-17/gh177-mktemp-fix-review.md`) asking Codex to independently confirm the GH-177 fix was solid. The 3-file production fix (`hq-hardening.sh`/`hq-promote.sh`/`hq-locator.sh`) was marked `[Pass]` every round — never challenged. The new regression test's own coverage was genuinely improved round over round, each finding verified against the real file before disposing: scan scope missed root-level scripts + `tools/` (r1); the guard check accepted any `||` as proof of validation, even non-aborting ones (r1→r2, two passes); missed same-line `;` chains and `local`/`declare`-prefixed assignments (r3); missed same-segment `&&`/`||` chains and an extensionless bash script (`bin/validate-relay-block`, real shebang, no `.sh` suffix) (r4). The relay hit its 5-round cap with one more valid finding still open (the guard check didn't verify a `-n`/`-d` test actually ABORTS on failure, not just mentions the var) and correctly escalated to the operator per its own ground rules rather than looping past the cap — that's the safety mechanism working as designed, not a failure. Operator chose to land one more fix rather than accept the gap or rescope: guard recognition is now fail-closed, requiring both non-empty AND is-directory tests, each confirmed to actually gate (`|| exit`/`|| return`/an aborting `{ }` block), tracked across same-line or split-line forms. Every fix in every round was verified two ways before landing: reconstructing the reviewer's exact evasion in a disposable scratch file (confirmed FAIL pre-fix, deleted after), and a full clean re-run (196/196 by the end). See the relay thread for the complete round-by-round record with `file:line` citations.

### Added
- **[GH-177](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/177) reopened and actually fixed — the `mktemp`-into-destructive-`EXIT`-trap repo wipe recurred, root cause was never patched the first time.** Closed 2026-07-08 as documentation-only; the unguarded `TMP="$(cd "$(mktemp -d)" && pwd -P)"; trap 'rm -rf "$TMP"' EXIT` idiom was still live verbatim in `test/hq-hardening.sh`, `test/hq-promote.sh`, and `test/hq-locator.sh`. Fired again 2026-07-17 when `validate.sh` ran under a sandboxed Claude Code `Bash` call: `mktemp -d` failed silently under the sandbox, `cd ""` succeeded and silently stayed at the cwd (the repo root — confirmed empirically, this is not a `cd` error), and the `EXIT` trap deleted the working tree plus `.git/objects`/`refs`/`HEAD`/`index` a second time (same survivors both times: `.git`, `.git/hooks`, and a couple of sandbox-protected paths). Recovered without upstream loss via `git init` (safe re-run) → `git fetch origin` (additive) → `git read-tree`/diff-before-touching-anything → `git checkout-index -a -f` from `origin/development` (`4fb2746`) → branch ref rewired → `git fsck` clean → stale `.git/worktrees/marathon-e-build` pruned, plus a Time Machine assist for the working tree. Fixed all 3 files with the guard the original issue already specified (exit-status + non-empty + is-directory check *before* the destructive trap, canonicalize only afterward). Added `test/mktemp-trap-guard.sh` — a static audit (same style as `test/marathon-root-audit.sh`) wired into `validate.sh` that fails on the exact historical idiom OR its split-across-lines twin (`X="$(mktemp -d)"` later re-captured via `X="$(cd "$X" && pwd)"`) anywhere in the repo, with no non-empty/is-directory guard in between. Verified both detection tiers against disposable scratch files (confirmed FAIL on the historical pattern, confirmed PASS — 191 files, 0 findings — on the fixed repo) before wiring it in, and narrowed two rounds of false positives along the way: a bare `WORK="$(mktemp -d)"` reaching `rm -rf "$WORK"` is empirically a safe no-op if mktemp fails (verified: `rm -rf ""` doesn't touch cwd) so isn't flagged, and a `cd "$VAR"` scoped inside an unrelated subshell/command-substitution that doesn't recapture a `pwd` result isn't flagged either — only a re-capturing `X="$(cd "$VAR" && pwd ...)"` is the real danger shape. A concurrent theory that a different repo's Codex relay-turn session (on a vendored `.xyz/` install) had somehow reached into this repo was investigated and ruled out (its `TICK_REPO_ROOT` never left its own repo; its own bug — `find-harness.sh --env` exporting the root one directory too deep — is real but unfiled separately). See [GH-177-MKTEMP-TRAP-REPO-WIPE.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-177-MKTEMP-TRAP-REPO-WIPE.md).

### Added
- **[GH-221](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/221) — explicit builder/orchestrator role split, closing a real doc gap causing session drift.** Some Claude Code sessions had started trying to use the Claude CLI as a marathon/relay builder. The code-level default was already correct (GH-212: `--builder` defaults to `codex`), but the only existing doc caveat was cost-framed ("don't assume it's free"), never role-framed — nothing stated that Claude Code is the orchestrator/reviewer, Agy/Codex CLI are the builders, and Claude CLI is an explicit user opt-in only. Worse, the vendored `skills/relay-xyz/SKILL.md` — the doc a vendored `.xyz/` install's own sessions actually read — had zero mention of a role split at all. Fixed both: added the explicit role statement to `AGENTS.md`'s "Repo-specific rails" and to `skills/relay-xyz/SKILL.md` (this file IS in `xyz-vendor.sh`'s `VENDOR_DIRS`, so it reaches every vendored `.xyz/` install on its next re-vendor — existing already-vendored copies need a manual re-vendor to pick it up immediately). Docs-only, no script behavior changed. `pdda.sh run` clean (pre-existing unrelated failures on GH-141/151/152/155 left untouched, out of scope).
- **PR #217 merged into `development` (not `main`) — widened the GH-216 "development is the WIP branch" policy to cover marathon-fired lanes too.** Originally opened against `main` per the existing GH-212 convention; retargeted mid-flight after confirming it was mechanically safe (`development` was a strict ancestor of the PR branch, 0 divergence). `gh pr merge --delete-branch` crashed mid-operation with a local `.git/index.lock` error (the PR merged fine on GitHub's side; the local branch ref just didn't fast-forward and the remote branch didn't get deleted) — recovered via `git merge --ff-only origin/development` plus a manual `git push origin --delete`. `AGENTS.md`'s rail updated: marathon/relay-fired lanes now branch off and PR into `development`, same as manual work; `main` no longer receives direct marathon merges. Local memory (`development-branch-is-wip-branch.md`) updated with both the policy widening and the `index.lock` recovery steps, so future sessions don't have to rediscover them.
- **Recent-issues (last 10 days) sweep — 4 new lanes triaged, captured, and fired as a combined marathon.** Reviewed every GitHub issue opened in the last 10 days for validity/reproducibility/completion; most were already captured or handled by prior passes. Four were new, still reproducible, and lane-sized: **[#208](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/208)** (`worktree-isolation.sh`'s moved-ROOT-HEAD race — reproduced 8/9 fail on 9 local runs, a genuine timing race not the "env-unfixable" the title speculated; queued into Marathon Plan F as Lane 12), **[#154](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/154)** (`utils/py/_marathon_plan_node.js` missing the GH-48 zone model that `utils/marathon-plan.sh` has — confirmed 0 references, no fix landed since filing; queued as Plan F Lane 13), **[#149](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/149)** (`marathon-drive.sh --require-clean` self-trips on its own `.relay-driver.lock` inside a linked worktree — confirmed still present by direct code read; new Marathon Plan G Lane 1), and **[#198](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/198)** Bug 2 only (`relay-drive.sh` has no fail-fast preflight check for a missing Setup-referenced artifact — Bug 1 of this issue turned out to already be fixed separately, commit `bee1abf`, `test/relay-commit-pathspec.sh` 9/9; new Plan G Lane 2). Wrote capture docs + Swarm Preflight Contracts for all four, verified each `ready` (exit 0) via `swarm-preflight.sh --gh-issue N --dry-run` individually (a combined `--gh-issue` bundle correctly refused with exit 7, "gate commands differ" — the 4 lanes have different `gate` tests, as expected for independent fixes). Added the two Plan-F-fitting lanes (208, 154) to `MARATHON-PLAN-2026-07-07-F-VALIDATE-FIXES.md`; authored a new `MARATHON-PLAN-2026-07-17-G-DRIVER-HARDENING.md` for the two driver-hardening bugs (149, 198), which don't fit Plan F's validate.sh/parity theme. Confirmed all 4 lanes' file sets are mutually disjoint (no write-set collisions), built a single 4-phase `MARATHON.yaml` (fully parallel, no `depends_on`) under `PROJECT/2-WORKING/MARATHON-2026-07-17-GH208-154-149-198/`, cut branch `marathon/gh208-154-149-198-2026-07-17`, and fired it via `marathon.sh --plan`. `#174`/`#215` (Plan F Lanes 10-11, ready from a prior pass) were deliberately left out of this fire — #174 shares `test/marathon-drive.sh` with #149, and firing both concurrently would collide; they remain available to fire separately. **All 4 phases Approved** (codex builder, agy reviewer, `bash validate.sh` gate each phase). Verified each fix by diffing the branch against `main` rather than trusting the "Approved" status alone: **#208**'s real root cause was NOT the suspected `relay-turn-lib.sh` race — it was a test-fixture timing bug (case 5's async write hadn't landed before case 6's cleanup), fixed with a 2s wait in the fixture; 8/8 clean repeated runs post-fix. **#154** ported the GH-48 zone model into `utils/py/_marathon_plan_node.js` and additionally had to wire `--zones-config` → `QUEUE_PLAN_ZONES_FILE` through `utils/marathon-plan.sh`'s `XYZ_PYTHON=1` dispatcher for the flag to survive the shell→Python→Node handoff; `test/marathon-plan.sh` 60/60 (2 new parity cases). **#149** resolves the driver lock via `git rev-parse --git-common-dir` in a linked worktree; `test/marathon-drive.sh` 105/105. **#198** Bug 2 added a Setup-artifact preflight check to `relay-drive.sh`; `test/relay-artifact-file.sh` 13/13. Full `bash validate.sh` post-marathon: 113/114 — only `relay-pkg-freshness.sh` red (the marathon's own commits touched `relay-turn-lib.sh`-adjacent scripts without rebuilding the packaged tarball), fixed by rebuilding it via `make-pkg.sh` and regenerating `ROADMAP-DASHBOARD.md`. The 4 capture docs' Status tables and checklists were NOT updated by the marathon builders despite the briefs asking for it — updated them by hand from the verified diffs instead of trusting the builder's self-report. `bash utils/pdda/pdda.sh run`: all checks passed after fixing two doc-hygiene errors this sweep introduced — a Status-table cell that broke the checker's naive pipe-split (a `||` inside inline code) and a missing `## Status` table in the new Plan G doc.
- **New `/10days` skill, dogfooded live on its first run — 11-14-day issue sweep, 5 of 6 candidates shipped.** Built `skills/10days/` (SKILL.md + `scan-issues.sh`/`find-doc.sh` deterministic helpers) to automate: scan a GH issue age window → verify each is still valid/reproducible/not-already-fixed via subagent fan-out → build + preflight a marathon → cut a branch and execute, unattended. Statically reviewed via `/consult --models agy` before running (caught and fixed: unsafe hand-rolled JSON in `find-doc.sh`, a `swarm-preflight.sh` multi-`--gh-issue` bundling misunderstanding, missing concurrent-marathon/behind-origin checks, no worktree isolation for parallel lanes). First live run (11-14 day window, chosen to not overlap the concurrent `#208/#154/#149/#198` sweep) scanned 16 issues, excluded 7 with commit/PR evidence (already-landed: #109, #111, #144, #96; gated-on-another-issue: #153; stale/paused: #97, #156, #157) and 2 more (#149, #154) for being actively claimed by that concurrent marathon — a collision the skill's own logic didn't catch on its own; caught by cross-referencing the other marathon's branch name live. Surfaced a real design gap along the way: `marathon-plan.sh` ranks the *entire* ROADMAP ledger, not just a sweep's own candidates, so its generated waves silently included the 2 already-claimed issues — fixed `SKILL.md` to require filtering to the sweep's own candidate set before firing anything. 6 issues survived triage; **5 shipped**: [#110](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/110) P2b (`test/roadmap-dashboard.sh` skip-guard + new top-level `run-tests.sh` entry point, `49c7b8d`), [#147](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/147) P2 (LM Studio lane threaded through the production Aider relay turn shim, byte-identical default OpenRouter path, 55/55 tests, `160c0fe`), [#151](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/151) P4 (cost-observability coverage-truth spike: Codex usage surface feasible, agy confirmed structurally cost-blind, Gemini relay-lane recommended for retirement, `7c77e4f`), [#152](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/152) P6 (`relay-drive.sh` now auto-surfaces the `tick analyze` cost block at end-of-run behind an env toggle, wired through the existing `EXIT` trap without touching the real exit code, `89d823a`), [#155](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/155) (3 new agy hardening regression cases — S2/S5/S8 — 54/54 own tests, `5f37954`). **[#141](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/141) deliberately shipped no fix** — investigated and correctly declined: no code-only signal can distinguish a peer session's concurrent edit from the agent's own off-lane self-escape (both produce identical porcelain diffs), and `rtl_enforce`'s revert is the documented backstop for a real, already-exploited self-escape vector (GH-22) — "fixing" this without an attribution signal would silently disable that protection. Recorded two non-detection follow-ups (recoverable backup-before-revert; document the don't-hand-edit-a-live-clone constraint) for an operator decision instead. Consolidated Wave 1 gate surfaced two real (non-flaky) cross-lane issues only visible once all 5 lanes merged together: a new unbaselined `credential-literal` finding from `#147`'s test fixture (`test/aider-turn.sh:322`, a dummy key — baselined, same class as the existing `test/deep-research.sh` entry) and a stale `relay-pkg.tar.gz` (two lanes each regenerated it for their own change via `make-pkg.sh`, but neither reflected the other's — refreshed once against the fully-merged tree). `worktree-isolation.sh` and `acorn-extract.sh` also failed in the gate; independently verified both fail identically against the pre-merge base commit in a scratch worktree (pre-existing GH-208 flaky race; missing `acorn` npm module) before ruling them out as unrelated. Live incident along the way: worktree directories (including the sweep's own and multiple lane worktrees) were deleted out from under running agents mid-`validate.sh` by an external, unrelated process — no committed work was lost (all recoverable from the branch), but it forced a switch to faster, targeted per-lane gates instead of the full suite to reduce the exposure window.
- **[GH-215](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/215) filed — GH-172 cutover-recommendation follow-up, plus Marathon Plan F made preflight-valid and a stale-issue finding.** Filed #215 for the residual `utils/py/consult.py` degraded-panel `SINGLE-MODEL — NOT RECONCILED` parity gap GH-172's cutover recommendation named. Added it to Marathon Plan F as Lane 11, alongside re-scoping Lane 10: **#174**'s actual code fix (the `utils/py/agy-turn.py` claim-before-launch guard) turned out to already be landed as a side effect of GH-172 Phase 0 (commit `7e9e683`, confirmed live) — commented on #174 with the evidence rather than closing it, since its own checklist's dedicated regression test is still missing; re-scoped to test-only (cx/risk/eff 2/1/2 → 1/1/1). While building Swarm Preflight Contracts for the plan's remaining lanes, **found Lanes 1-9 (umbrella #170) are themselves STALE**: `swarm-preflight.sh --gh-issue 170` returns exit 4, and all 9 originally-failing tests now independently verify green (incl. 5x repeats of the two confirmed-flaky lanes; full `validate.sh` shows only the pre-existing `#208` red repo-wide) — root cause of the flip unconfirmed (no commit explains it, unlike #174), commented on #170 recommending closure rather than closing unilaterally. Promoted `GH-170-VALIDATE-FAILING-TESTS.md`, `GH-174-AGY-PY-CLAIM-GUARD.md` (both `1-INBOX` → `2-WORKING`) and `MARATHON-PLAN-2026-07-07-F-VALIDATE-FIXES.md` (`4-MISC` → `2-WORKING`, required for `marathon.sh --plan`'s GH-212 plan-location guard), added Swarm Preflight Contracts to all three plus `GH-147-LM-STUDIO.md` (scoped to its Phase 2). `swarm-preflight.sh --dry-run` confirmed ready (exit 0) for #174, #215, and #147. `bash utils/pdda/pdda.sh run`: all checks passed.
- **[GH-172](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/172) — vendored harness root-semantics audit: 4-phase marathon merged via [PR #214](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/pull/214); #172 closed.** Phase 0 (already-shipped root/claim parity fixes) plus 4 new phases, codex builder/agy reviewer, `bash validate.sh` gate: **Phase 1 (Bash audit)** checked `marathon-drive.sh`, `relay-drive.sh`, `marathon-agent.sh`, `relay-turn-lib.sh`, `aider-turn.sh`, `consult.sh`, `relay-loop.sh`, `watchdog.sh`, `runner.sh`, and `swarm-preflight.sh` against the three-root contract — found and fixed a real gap in `relay-automation/consult.sh` (stale `${TICK_BIN:-$ROOT/bin/tick}` fallback broke the tick-binary/coordination-root split in a vendored/root-split run), every other file verified clean. **Phase 2 (Python audit)** checked `marathon_drive.py`, `relay_drive.py`, `rtl.py`, `aider-turn.py`, and `consult.py` for parity with the hardened Bash behavior — found and fixed the matching gap in `utils/py/consult.py`, plus an unrelated `utils/py/relay_drive.py` warning-parity gap (same-repo vs. cross-repo uncommitted relay-file visibility warning didn't match the Bash split). **Phase 3** extended `test/marathon-drive.sh` with vendored `.xyz` consumer-repo E2E coverage (builder + reviewer legs, both Bash and `XYZ_PYTHON=1`). **Phase 4** wrote the durable root contract into the capture doc and an explicit cutover recommendation: safe to cut a stable Bash branch now; **not yet safe** to switch `main` to Python-default — `utils/py/consult.py` still lacks the Bash degraded-panel `SINGLE-MODEL — NOT RECONCILED` stamping, keeping `XYZ_PYTHON=1 bash test/consult.sh` red — filed as [#215](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/215), see its own entry below. Full `validate.sh` green except the pre-existing, tracked [#208](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/208) environment red — independently re-verified outside the marathon's own gate. Capture doc + findings: [GH-172-VENDORED-ROOT-AUDIT.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-172-VENDORED-ROOT-AUDIT.md) · [GH-172-BASH-AUDIT-FINDINGS.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-172-BASH-AUDIT-FINDINGS.md) · [GH-172-PYTHON-AUDIT-FINDINGS.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-172-PYTHON-AUDIT-FINDINGS.md) · [GH-172-CUTOVER-RECOMMENDATION.md](PROJECT/3-COMPLETED/GH-172-CUTOVER-RECOMMENDATION.md).
- **GH-213/209/203 — 3-lane marathon, all Approved, first real dogfood of the GH-212 conventions.** All three built from the last-7-days issue sweep, each with a capture doc + Swarm Preflight Contract under `PROJECT/2-WORKING/`. **GH-213**: `find-harness.sh --check` gains a case-collision detector (two git-tracked paths differing only by case — a macOS `core.ignorecase=true` landmine distinct from GH-17's narrower fix), `test/find-harness.sh` 20/20. **GH-209**: scope narrowed from the issue's full architectural ask (risks breaking GH-206's zero-config goal) down to a static audit (`test/marathon-root-audit.sh`, 13/13, new) — codex's own build turn found and fixed the ACTUAL root cause behind a gate false-negative hit earlier in this same marathon: `MARATHON_LANE_NS` leaking from a live `marathon-drive.sh` process into a nested `test/marathon.sh` invocation's own rendered paths. **GH-203**: scope narrowed to a non-destructive `swarm-preflight.sh` preflight warning for a stale `.git/index.lock` (`lsof`-based, advisory-only, never changes the verdict), `test/swarm-preflight.sh` 94/94 (+7 new cases). Two recoveries needed along the way, both cleanly diagnosed and fixed rather than papered over: (1) gh213's own shared `--pre-advance-cmd` nested `test/marathon.sh` inside the still-live outer marathon process, causing the false-negative above — worked around by verifying that suite standalone instead of nested; (2) gh209's agy review tripped the known, already-tracked GH-183 isolation-breach false positive (content-scan flagging a legitimate mention of "the real repo root") — the actual review was independently re-verified as correct and its Approved content committed by hand after re-running every affected test suite standalone. Also found and fixed in passing: the phase-brief `.md` files this marathon wrote under `PROJECT/2-WORKING/` tripped PDDA's blanket frontmatter/status-table enforcement (every `.md` there is scanned as an active-doc capture) — added minimal frontmatter + `roadmap_exempt: true` to mark them as consumed builder-input, not capture docs. Full `validate.sh` green throughout except the pre-existing, tracked [#208](https://github.com/Claude-AI-Tools-Ventura-County/xyz-3-agents-swarm/issues/208) environment red.
